Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the probabilities structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 335. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 607 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 335 + O(1/n^607). Summing over all modes and applying the probabilities convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
